<h3><a href="https://www.threads.com/@saars.foto/post/DgKy_JioK1F">Looking Back Wednesday 👀 Today&#x27;s prompt is things that look back at you - eyes and faces hiding in the everyday. My hero is a Jeep headlight doing a very good impression of an angry eye. Show me ONE photo of something staring back: headlights, windows, a knot in the wood, a face in the clouds. Replying is consent: • Top 5 featured tomorrow on Threads, pinned • Top 3 also in the Instagram reel • Full credit to the author everywhere • Standouts may join a best-of-the-week reel</a></h3>
